# FORMULA REGISTER — reconstructed from source evidence

**Purpose** one row per implementation site, with the evidence behind every field.
**Source** extraction from the repository at `545d212`, plus its import/export graph.
**Generated** by the units engine under the register reconstruction rule.
**Status** AUTHORITATIVE. The register it replaces is preserved whole at
`FORMULA-REGISTER-PRE-RECONSTRUCTION.md`; every changed classification is listed in
`REGISTER-RECONSTRUCTION.md`.

**This register carries no truncation.** The previous builder capped the variable list
at six entries and the writer cut cells at ~90 characters, so the document could not be
re-derived from itself (F-39). Both caps are gone; equations and variable lists are
written in full.

---

## 1. What 2,429 actually is

2,429 is the number of IMPLEMENTATION SITES the extractor finds in the engineering
modules. It is not the number of engineering formulas, and this file does not call it
one.

| kind | rows | share |
|---|---|---|
| **C1** distinct engineering relation | 1288 | 53.0% |
| **C7** duplicated implementation | 422 | 17.4% |
| **C5** geometric construction | 369 | 15.2% |
| **C3** intermediate arithmetic | 263 | 10.8% |
| **C4** unit conversion | 78 | 3.2% |
| **C6** UI/display calculation | 9 | 0.4% |
| **total sites** | **2429** | 100% |

**Distinct engineering relations: 1288 retained sites, 1193 distinct
expression shapes among them.** C1 counts the first occurrence of a shape PER FILE, so
the honest statement is a bracket, not a point:

> between 1193 and 1288 distinct engineering relations, implemented at
> 2429 sites, of which 422 are duplicate implementations.

## 2. The classification rule

Unchanged from the original register, and reproduced there in full: applied in order,
first match wins — C6 UI/display, C5 geometric construction, C4 unit conversion,
C3 intermediate arithmetic, C7 duplicated implementation, C1 what remains. The
classification itself was NOT re-derived in this reconstruction; only the units were.

## 3. Unit verification — what this register now claims

| class | rows | meaning | confidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| DECLARED | 165 | the source states the unit, or an anchored name does | HIGH |
| DERIVED | 198 | computed by dimensional propagation through the expression | MEDIUM |
| FROM-SIGNATURE | 0 | computed through a called function's derived return unit | MEDIUM |
| BACK-INFERRED | 0 | solved algebraically from a known output and known operands | LOW |
| AMBIGUOUS | 135 | the name admits several dimensions; NOT resolved from context | NONE |
| CONFLICT | 6 | two operands that must agree do not | NONE |
| UNRESOLVED | 784 | no route reaches it | NONE |
| **total** | **1288** | | |

**Output units resolved for 363 of 1288 relations (28.2%).**

The register this replaces recorded 190 (14.8 %). That figure must not be compared
directly: **61 of those 190 were wrong** (F-38), so the defensible prior figure is
**129 (10.0 %)**. Against that, the reconstruction is a 2.8x improvement, and every
row now carries the evidence for its own unit.

**Input-variable units: 1048 of 3934 (26.6%)**, with
853 declared AMBIGUOUS rather than guessed. The previous denominator (5,055)
counted built-ins such as `Math.PI` and object roots such as `p.` as variables that
merely lacked a unit; they are not variables and are excluded here.

### Fields carried per row

| field | how it is filled |
|---|---|
| formula ID, file, function, line | extraction, verified against the live source |
| equation | the source line verbatim, string literals blanked, **untruncated** |
| inputs / input units | identifiers on the right-hand side that are physical quantities; built-ins and object roots excluded |
| output units | the reconstruction, by the evidence order below |
| source / source location | the nearest citation or tag to the line |
| assumption type | from the tag; else UNKNOWN |
| implementation status | IMPLEMENTED for every row — the code exists and runs |
| unit verification | the class above |
| independent verification | **NO for every row.** Phase 3 recomputes ~20 quantities; which 20 is not recorded per formula, and a domain verdict is not a per-formula recomputation |
| validation status | NOT VALIDATED — Phase 2 |
| regression status | whether a registered harness imports the file (FILE-level, not line-level) |
| current finding | a dimensional conflict on the line, else NONE |
| provenance | the evidence chain behind the output unit |
| confidence | HIGH stated · MEDIUM derived · LOW back-inferred · NONE otherwise |

**Evidence order for a unit** — 1 explicit source statement, 2 an anchored name,
3 dimensional derivation, 4 conservative back-inference, 5 otherwise UNKNOWN or
AMBIGUOUS. A naming heuristic never overrides source evidence.
